Let's design multi-phase:
0. Load initial, project, compute c. best.
1. Continuous projected gradient/soft (Alpha schedule) for e.g. 25% time. Use fast objective/grad. Boundary projection bisection.
2. Pair coordinate descent/1D from best for 30% time. Use heuristic pairs and maybe all pairs in low-dimensional active set.
3. Binary: round best to binary (exact top n/2 if not exact), run binary local search (swap acceptance) with more exhaustive strategy. Then continuous polish from binary and from parent.
4. Multi-resolution: try n= parent (+maybe round to even). Also n=200,300,400,600. For each, upsample best, run shortened continuous+pair. Keep best.
5. At deadline return best h, c direct.

But code complexity and time. Need avoid over-time. Use deadline checks. We can set conservative constants. Since single CPU, unknown speed. Need not run too much that times out. Use time checks every loop.

Need exact project_feasible: ensure sum close. Implement robust:
```
def project_feasible(z, n):
    h=np.clip(z,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(100):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          # add to room, prefer interior? Use water-filling: h_new=clip(z-lambda,0,1) with sum target
```
Better implement exact projection via bisection on lambda for Lagrangian:
Find λ such that sum clip(z_i - λ,0,1)=target. If z sum target, λ maybe. Bisection over λ range [z.min()-1, z.max()+1]. Since sum monotone decreasing in λ. After get h, residual diff small; adjust over indices where 0<h<1 (or all) by diff/count. Since h may be at bounds if many; but target within [0,n], there is λ achieving almost exact. If residual >1e-13, distribute to all entries with slack (0<h<1) count interior; if none (all 0/1), adjust one at boundary? For binary exact sum integer, no residual. For continuous, interior likely. Feasible projection via bisection: implement exactly. Let's design robust and fast.
```
def project_feasible(z,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(z,float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    s=h.sum()
    diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-12:
       return h
    # water-filling bisection
    lo = h.min()-1.0
    hi = h.max()+1.0
    # want f(lambda)=sum clip(z-lambda)-target = 0, z original? Use h? should use z before clip? If use z, can recover sum. Use zc=np.clip(z, -1e-6,1+1e-6)? We want project z onto bounds and sum. Use z0=np.asarray(z,float); lo=min(z0)-1, hi=max(z0)+1. But if z0 all 0 and target>0, hi=1, ok. Need f(lo) because lambda negative large -> all 1 sum n-target positive; f(hi) negative.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       val=np.clip(z0 - mid,0,1).sum()
       if val > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    h=np.clip(z0-lam,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    # residual
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       interior=(h>1e-10)&(h<1-1e-10)
       if interior.sum()>0:
          h[interior]+=diff/interior.sum()
       else:
          # if all at bounds, adjust one as much as possible
          if diff>0:
             idx=np.argmax(1-h) # none? choose 0
          else: idx=np.argmin(h)
          h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

At lo, z0-lo >=1 all -> sum n; target <= n (since n even target n/2). f>0. At hi, sum 0 f<0. Good. 60 iters maybe overkill; 45 enough. Use 50.
Potential sum residual after correction may violate bounds if interior adjustment diff exceeds slack. But residual should be <1e-10 if bisection tolerance (hi-lo ~ (range)/2^50 ~1e-14). Good. Use 60.

```
def optimize_t(low, high, c_active, A_active, c_extra, A_extra):
   if high - low < 1e-12: return (low+high)/2
   gr = (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
   x1 = low + (1-gr)*(high-low)
   x2 = low + gr*(high-low)
   f1 = phi(x1,...)
   f2 = phi(x2,...)

      else: low=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=low+gr*(high-low); f2=phi(...)
   return (low+high)/2
```

```
grad = np.zeros(n)
L=2n-1
for p in active_idx:
    d = n-1-p
    w= weight (1)
    # grad_x = I(x+d valid) - h[x+d] - h[x-d] valid
    plus x range: start=max(0,-d), end=min(n-1,n-1-d)
    grad[start:end+1] += 1 - h[start+d:end+d+1]
    minus x range: start=max(0,d), end=min(n-1,n-1+d) (because x-d 0..n-1)
    grad[start:end+1] -= h[start-d:end-d+1]
# average/normalize
v = -grad; v -= v.mean()
```

`smooth_unnorm(h, alpha)`:
```
def smooth_unnorm(h, alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h,float)
    n=len(h)
    c = np.convolve(h, (1.0-h)[::-1], mode='full')
    m = c.max()
    if alpha <= 0:
       return m, np.zeros_like(h)
    z = alpha*(c-m)
    # for numerical, z can be large negative; exp underflow ok
    expz = np.exp(z)
    s = expz.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       # fallback one-hot max
       w = np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0
    else:
       w = expz / s
    g = weighted_gradient(h, w)
    return float(m + np.log(s)/alpha), g
```
If alpha large, z for nonmax may be -inf? `np.exp(-inf)=0`. If alpha*diff underflows to -inf? okay. If alpha is inf? Not.

`weighted_gradient(h, w)`:
Input w length L normalized sum 1 (not important). Use B = w[::-1]. Need if n small. Implement with `np.convolve` or `fftconvolve`? Let's use `np.convolve` for n up to maybe 800 to avoid FFT overhead; for larger maybe `scipy.signal.fftconvolve`. We can choose:

```
def convolve2(a,b):
   if len(a)*len(b) <= 2000000: return np.convolve(a,b,'full')
   else: from scipy.signal import fftconvolve; return fftconvolve(a,b)
```

`weighted_gradient`:
```
def weighted_gradient(h, weights):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1
    B = weights[::-1]
    pref = np.empty(L+1); pref[0]=0.0; np.cumsum(B, out=pref[1:])
    starts = np.arange(n-1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    ends = starts + (n-1)
    V = pref[ends+1] - pref[starts]
    D2 = np.convolve(h, B, mode='full')
    S2 = D2[n-1:2*n-1]
    Hrev = h[::-1]
    D1 = np.convolve(Hrev, B, mode='full')
    S1 = D1[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return V - S1 - S2
```
Check slice `2*n-1` exclusive? Python end=2*n-1 gives last index 2n-2 length n? n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n? (2n-2 - (n-1)+1)=n. yes. For n=2, end=3 indices1,2 length2.
V starts: n-1, n-2,...0. ends = starts+n-1 =2n-2...n-1. pref[end+1] start. Good.
If weights sum not 1, grad scales. Fine.
Need ensure weights nonnegative finite; if sum zero return zeros.

Potential issue: `np.cumsum(B, out=pref[1:])` works? pref dtype float. yes.

`direction_from_active(h,c,active,mode?)`:
```
def active_gradient(h, c, active):
   n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n); L=2*n-1
   if len(active)==0: return grad
   # weights: uniform or slack weights
   # maybe active values, C=c.max(); wexp = 1/(max(C-c[active],1e-3)+0.01)
   vals = c[active]
   w = 1.0/(np.maximum(vals? actually slack = C - vals) + 1e-6)
   # normalize? Not necessary for direction magnitude; use sum.
   for p,wt in zip(active,w):
      d = n-1-p
      # plus range
      if d >=0:
         start=0; end=n-1-d
      else: start=-d; end=n-1
      grad[start:end+1] += wt * (1.0 - h[start+d:end+d+1])
      # minus range: x-d valid => x from max(0,d) to min(n-1,n-1+d)
      if d >=0:
         start2=d; end2=n-1
      else: start2=0; end2=n-1+d
      grad[start2:end2+1] -= wt * h[start2-d:end2-d+1]
   return grad
```
For d=0, plus start0 end n-1: grad +=1-h; minus start0 end n-1: grad -=h => 1-2h. Good.

```
def pair_linear_delta(h,i,j,n):
    L=2*n-1
    A = np.zeros(L)
    # i plus
    a=i; end=a+n-1
    p = np.arange(a, end+1) # avoid allocation? We can slice and reversed h values.
    # contribution: 1 - h[a+n-1-p]. For p from a to a+n-1, index = a+n-1-p runs from n-1 down to a.
    # Use slice: vals = h[a:n][::-1]? Let's derive: p=a -> idx=n-1; p=a+n-1 -> idx=a. So h[a:n][::-1] length n.

```

```
A = np.zeros(L)
A[i:i+n] += 1.0 - h[::-1]
A[j:j+n] -= 1.0 - h[::-1]
```

Pair search loop structured pseudocode:
```
def pair_search(h, n, c, deadline, max_sweeps=1000, seed=123, K=48, Q=24, tol=1e-13):
    h = h.copy(); c = c.copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    L=2*n-1
    best_c = c.max()
    best_h = h.copy()
    active = top_active(c, K)
    c_act = c[active].copy()
    A = np.zeros(L)
    # maybe precompute active_extra? no
    no_improve = 0
    accept_count=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       if no_improve > 3000? maybe return
       # direction from active
       grad = active_gradient(h, c, active, best_c) ?
       v = -grad; v -= v.mean()
       # pools
       # Combine deterministic + random
       pos, neg = build_pools(v, h, rng, Q)
       # Also if n<=Q? loops all
       for i in pos:
          for j in neg:
             if i==j: continue
             hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
             low = max(-hi, hj-1.0)
             high = min(hj, 1.0-hi)
             if high - low < 1e-12: continue
             # fill A
             A.fill(0.0)
             A[i:i+n] += r0
             A[j:j+n] -= r0
             A[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
             A[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
             # if active? need c_act maybe current
             p1 = n-1 - (j-i); p2 = n-1 + (j-i)
             t = optimize_t(low, high, c_act, A[active], c[p1?], A[p1?], ...)
             # full verify
             A.add? Use np.add(c, t*A, out=buf_new)
             new_c = c + t*A
             if p1==p2? no
             new_c[p1] += t*t
             if p2 != p1: new_c[p2] += t*t
             newmax = new_c.max()
             if newmax < best_c - tol:
                h[i]=hi+t; h[j]=hj-t
                c = new_c
                best_c = newmax
                best_h = h.copy()
                c_act = c[active] # recompute vector
                no_improve=0; accept_count++
                if accept_count%50==0:
                   # periodic recompute c and active
                   c = c_unnorm_direct(h); best_c=c.max(); ...
             else:
                no_improve +=1
          if time.time()>deadline: break
       if no_improve > max(500, len(pos)*len(neg)*?):
           # maybe shuffle random and continue once, else break
```

`phi_t`:
```
def _phi(t, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2):
    val = np.max(c_act + t*A_act) if c_act.size else -np.inf
    t2 = t*t
    v1 = c1 + t*A1 + t2
    if v1 > val: val = v1
    v2 = c2 + t*A2 + t2
    if v2 > val: val = v2
    return val
```

2. `proj_feasible(z,n)`: Need if z length maybe not n? assume. Newton? Implement bisection robust and not too slow. It will be called often (after SLSQP, after multi-res). Not in pair_search (mass conserved). Could optimize if z already close:
```
def project_feasible(z,n):
    x=np.asarray(z,float)
    target=0.5*n
    s=x.sum()
    # clip if close? We'll always project.
    lo=float(np.min(x))-1.0
    hi=float(np.max(x))+1.0
    # If x within bounds, maybe use simple bisection with z0=x
    z0=x
    for _ in range(45):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        val=np.clip(z0-mid,0.0,1.0)
        if val.sum() > target:
            lo=mid
        else:
            hi=mid
    h=np.clip(z0-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff != 0:
        interior=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
        cnt=interior.sum()
        if cnt>0:
            h[interior] += diff/cnt
        else:
            # adjust all with small eps
            h += diff/n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If z has values outside, lo/hi range based on min/max. If all z=0, lo=-1,hi=1; bisection finds sum target. If n even target maybe large. Good.
But 45 iterations each projection. Could be called many times e.g. after each SLSQP call maybe dozens. okay. Could speed by alternative if x already within bounds and sum diff small:

```

   h=np.clip(x,0,1); diff=target-h.sum(); if abs(diff)<0.5: h[interior]+=diff/cnt; return h
```

3. `c_unnorm_direct(h)`.

```
def c_unnorm_direct(h):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    return np.convolve(h, (1-h)[::-1], 'full')
```

4. `smooth_unnorm(h, alpha)`.
Need if alpha=0? Use max and uniform gradient? If alpha=0, objective m, gradient not defined; skip. Use alpha>0.
```
def smooth_unnorm(h,alpha):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    c=c_unnorm_direct(h)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 1e-8:
       return m, np.zeros_like(h)
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    # avoid overflow: z <=0 theoretically, but due round maybe small positive; subtract max z
    zm=np.max(z)
    expz=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=expz.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0
    else: w=expz/s
    g=weighted_gradient(h,w)
    obj=m + np.log(s + 1e-300)/alpha
    return obj,g
```
Since c-m<=0, zm=0 unless round; fine.

6. SLSQP wrapper:
```
def run_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    global _OPT_ALPHA,_OPT_TARGET
    _OPT_ALPHA=float(alpha); _OPT_TARGET=0.5*n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_constraint_fun,'jac':_constraint_jac}]
    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'disp':False}
    # maybe maxls? If line search fails, result still best? Use res.x
    res = minimize(_obj_jac, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=True, options=options)
    return project_feasible(res.x,n)
```

Pool details:
```
def build_pools(v,h,rng,Q,n):
    Q = int(Q)
    Q = max(1, min(Q, n))
    pos_parts=[]; neg_parts=[]
    if Q < n:
       pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, -Q)[-Q:])
       neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, :?)) # np.argpartition(v, Q)[:Q]
       low = np.argpartition(h, Q)[:Q]
       high = np.argpartition(h, n-Q)[-Q:]
    else:
       allidx=np.arange(n)
       pos_parts.append(allidx); neg_parts.append(allidx)
    pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, Q)[:Q]) # low h, can increase
    neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, n-Q)[-Q:]) # high h, can decrease
    if n>Q:
       pos_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False))
       neg_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False))
    pos = np.unique(np.concatenate(pos_parts))
    neg = np.unique(np.concatenate(neg_parts))
    return pos, neg
```

```
def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    if n_new == n_old: return h.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    # piecewise constant nearest? np.interp linear may smooth. For step function maybe linear okay; projection then optimizer can sharpen. Or use block averaging for down, for up linear.
    hh = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return project_feasible(hh,n_new)
```

Implementation of `run`:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    safe=budget_s-6
    if safe <=0: safe = 1.0
    end = t0 + safe
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # load parent
    try:
       h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       h = _fallback_initial(seed)
    n = len(h)
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
    best_unn = float(c.max()); best_norm = best_unn*2.0/n
    best_h = h.copy(); best_n = n
    # Phase parent SLSQP
    if time.time() < end:
       sl_time = t0 + 0.30*safe
       best_h,best_norm,best_n = slsqp_polish(best_h,best_n,sl_time,seed,alphas=[10,30,100,300,1000])
       c = c_unnorm_direct(best_h)
    # Phase pair
    if time.time() < end:
       pair_end = min(end - 5, t0 + 0.60*safe)
       best_h,best_norm,best_n = pair_polish(best_h,best_n,pair_end,seed,alphas? none)
    # Phase SLSQP high
    if time.time() < end - 20:
       second_slesq_end = min(end-20, t0+0.70*safe)
       best_h,best_norm,best_n = slsqp_polish(...)
    # Phase pair again
    if time.time() < end - 10:
       best_h,best_norm,best_n = pair_polish(best_h,best_n,min(end-10,...),seed+1)
    # Multi-res
    if time.time() < end - 30:
       factor list; for each try if time > 60:
           optimize res and update
    # final pair
    if time.time() < end - 3:
       best_h,best_norm,best_n = pair_polish(...,end-2)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, final_c, best_n
```

```
    hs,cs = pair_search(best_h,n,c,deadline,seed,K)
```

Let's write build_pools top-level:
```
def build_pools(v,h,rng,Q,n):
    pos_parts=[]; neg_parts=[]
    Q = max(1, min(int(Q), n))
    if Q < n:
        pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, -Q)[-Q:])
        neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, Q)[:Q])
        pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, Q)[:Q])
        neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, n-Q)[-Q:])
        if Q*2 < n:
            pos_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False))
            neg_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False))
    else:
        allidx=np.arange(n)
        pos_parts.append(allidx)
        neg_parts.append(allidx)
    pos = np.unique(np.concatenate(pos_parts))
    neg = np.unique(np.concatenate(neg_parts))
    if len(pos)*len(neg) > 6000: # cap? 
       # To avoid huge, subsample
       if len(pos)>40: pos=rng.choice(pos,40,replace=False)
       if len(neg)>40: neg=rng.choice(neg,40,replace=False)
    return pos,neg
```
Capping to 40 each => max 1600. Good.

`optimize_t` implementation no closures:
```
def eval_pair_phi(t, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2):
    if c_act.size:
        val = float(np.max(c_act + t*A_act))
    else:
        val = -np.inf
    t2 = t*t
    v1 = c1 + t*A1 + t2
    if v1 > val: val = v1
    v2 = c2 + t*A2 + t2
    if v2 > val: val = v2
    return val
```
This allocates. For K=64 and 30 iterations, okay.

```
        return 0.5*(low+high)

    a=low; b=high
    x1 = a + (1-gr)*(b-a)
    x2 = a + gr*(b-a)
    f1=eval...; f2=eval...
    for _ in range(iterations):
       if f1 < f2:
```

`run_slsqp`:
```
def run_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    global _OPT_ALPHA,_OPT_N,_OPT_TARGET,_OPT_DEADLINE
    h0 = project_feasible(h,n)
    _OPT_ALPHA=float(alpha); _OPT_N=n; _OPT_TARGET=0.5*n; _OPT_DEADLINE=deadline
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_constraint_fun,'jac':_constraint_jac}]
    try:
       res = minimize(_obj_jac, h0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=True, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-15,'disp':False})
       x1=res.x
    except Exception:
       x1=h0
    _OPT_DEADLINE=0.0
    return project_feasible(x1,n)
```
If `TimeLimit` raised, x1=h0. Good.

```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline,seed,alphas, maxiter=None):
    best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    if maxiter is None: maxiter=...
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for rep in range(2): # if time
      for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
        h_try=best_h
        if rep>0:
           h_try=project_feasible(best_h + 0.02*rng.randn(n), n)
        if compute_c5(h_try,n) < best_c: update (noise)
        if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
        h1=run_slsqp(h_try,n,alpha,maxiter, deadline - 2)
        if h1 is None: continue
        c1=compute_c5(h1,n)
        if c1 < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c=c1; best_h=h1.copy()
        # maybe if no improvement, break alpha? continue
      else: continue
      break
    return best_h,best_c,n
```

`pair_search` more code. Let's draft in detail.
```
def pair_search(h, n, deadline, seed, K=None, Q=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
    best_c = float(c.max()); if not finite -> recompute
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_norm = best_c * (2.0/n)
    L=2*n-1
    if K is None: K=min(64, max(24, n//5))
    K=min(K,L)
    if Q is None: Q=min(16, max(6, n//15))
    Q=min(Q,n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    active = top_active(c,K)
    r0 = 1.0 - h[::-1]
    work = np.empty(L)
    A = np.empty(L)
    tol_norm = 1e-12
    empty_sweeps=0
    long_no=0
    max_sweeps=10000
    accept_count=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       if empty_sweeps >= 3: break
       if long_no > 20000: break
       # recompute active every sweep
       cmax = float(c.max())
       if cmax > best_c + 1e-9: # shouldn't
          best_c=cmax; best_norm=...; best_h=h.copy()?
       active = top_active(c,K)
       c_act = c[active]  # view
       grad = active_gradient(h,c,max(cmax,1e-12),active)
       v = -grad
       v -= float(v.mean())
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
          v = np.zeros(n)
       pos, neg = build_pools(v,h,rng,Q,n)
       sweep_improved=False
       # maybe deterministic loops first then random pairs
       for i in pos:
          hi0=h[i]
          # if hi0 near 1 and pos is receiver? interval high may be low. Still.
          for j in neg:
             if i == j: continue
             if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
             hi = h[i]; hj = h[j]
             low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
             high = min(hj, 1.0 - hi)
             if high - low <= 1e-13: continue
             A.fill(0.0)
             A[i:i+n] += r0
             A[j:j+n] -= r0
             A[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
             A[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
             dy = j - i
             p1 = n - 1 - dy
             p2 = n - 1 + dy
             # extract active/A active
             A_act = A[active]
             c1 = c[p1]; A1 = A[p1]
             c2 = c[p2]; A2 = A[p2]
             t = optimize_t(low, high, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2, 20)
             # clamp
             if t < low: t = low
             if t > high: t = high
             if abs(t) < 1e-15: continue
             t2 = t*t
             np.multiply(A, t, out=work)
             np.add(c, work, out=work)
             work[p1] += t2
             if p2 != p1: work[p2] += t2
             newmax = float(work.max())
             if newmax * (2.0/n) < best_norm - tol_norm:
                 hi_new = hi + t; hj_new = hj - t
                 if hi_new < -1e-12 or hi_new > 1+1e-12 or hj_new < -1e-12 or hj_new > 1+1e-12:
                     h[i] = min(1.0,max(0.0,hi_new)); h[j]=min(1.0,max(0.0,hj_new))
                     c = c_unnorm_direct(h); best_c=float(c.max()); best_norm=best_c*2.0/n; r0=1.0-h[::-1]; active=top_active(c,K); best_h=h.copy(); best_norm_update if better
                 else:
                     h[i]=hi_new; h[j]=hj_new
                     np.copyto(c, work)
                     r0[n-1-i] -= t
                     r0[n-1-j] += t
                     best_c = newmax; best_norm = newmax*(2.0/n); best_h = h.copy()
                     accept_count += 1
                     sweep_improved=True
                     if accept_count % 50 == 0:
                         c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
                         mc=float(c.max())
                         best_norm=mc*2.0/n
                         # it should be <= best_norm + eps; if larger? set
                         if mc > best_c: best_c=mc
                         active = top_active(c,K); r0=1.0-h[::-1]
                     long_no=0
             else:
                 long_no += 1
          if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
       # random trials
       if time.time() < deadline - 0.5:
          nr = max(20, 4*Q)
          for _ in range(nr):
             if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
             i = rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
             if i==j: continue
             ... same trial ...
       if sweep_improved:
          empty_sweeps=0
       else:
          empty_sweeps += 1
       # if long_no > 10000 and not improved? break? if long_no>20000 break
    # final ensure
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    final_norm = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    # maybe if current h better
    cur_norm = compute_c5(h,n)
    if cur_norm < final_norm:
       best_h=h; final_norm=cur_norm
    return best_h, final_norm, n
```

`active_gradient`:
```
def active_gradient(h,c,active):
    n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n)
    if len(active)==0: return grad
    cmax = float(np.max(c[active])) # or c.max
    vals = c[active]
    # weights: maybe uniform
    w = np.ones_like(vals)/len(vals)
    # Could boost near max: w = 1/(cmax-vals+0.05); w/=sum. Let's use boost.
    slack = np.maximum(cmax - vals, 1e-6)
    w = 1.0/(slack + 0.05)
    s=w.sum()
    if s>0: w /= s
    for p,wt in zip(active,w):
       d = n-1-int(p)
       ...
    return grad
```

Uniform may be more stable. Use uniform to avoid focusing too much. Maybe top K all equally. Use `w = np.full(len(active), 1.0/len(active))`. Simpler.

`top_active`:
```
def top_active(c,K):
   L=len(c); K=int(min(K,L))
   if K <=0: return np.array([],dtype=int)
   if K >= L: return np.arange(L)
   return np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
```

`slsqp_polish`: if deadline close, skip. It should not modify input? It copies.
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline,seed=None,alphas=None,maxiter=None):
    best_h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(1234 if seed is None else seed)
    if alphas is None: alphas=[10,50,200,800]
    if maxiter is None:
       if n <= 300: maxiter=150
       elif n<=600: maxiter=80
       else: maxiter=50
    # maybe two reps
    for rep in range(2):
      for alpha in alphas:
         if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
         h_try = best_h
         if rep > 0:
            scale = 0.01/(1+rep)
            h_try = project_feasible(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), n)
            c_try=compute_c5(h_try,n)
            if c_try < best_c:
                best_c=c_try; best_h=h_try.copy()
                if time.time() > deadline-8: break
         h1 = run_slsqp(h_try,n,alpha,maxiter, deadline - 2)
         if h1 is not None:
            c1 = compute_c5(h1,n)
            if c1 < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c=c1; best_h=h1.copy()
      if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
    return best_h,best_c,n
```
If no parent and starting random, noisy SLSQP maybe. But parent.
Maybe include after SLSQP a pair polish in main, not here to avoid long.

`pair_polish` simple:
```
def pair_polish(h,n,deadline,seed):
    if time.time() > deadline: return h, compute_c5(h,n), n
    best_h,best_c,n = pair_search(h,n,deadline,seed)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```
No need extra.

```
def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new):
   h=np.clip(...)
   if n_new==n_old: return project_feasible(h,n_new)
   if max(h)==min(h): return np.full(n_new,0.5)
   x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old,endpoint=False)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new,endpoint=False)
   hh=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h,left=0,right=0)
   return project_feasible(hh,n_new)
```

Let's outline `run` with phases and time slices concretely.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    safe = max(10.0, budget_s - 6.0)
    end = t0 + safe
    # parent
    try: h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception: h = None
    if h is None or len(h)==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       n=250; h=np.full(n,0.5)
    n=len(h)
    if n < 4: n=250; h=np.full(n,0.5)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h; best_norm=compute_c5(h,n); best_n=n
    # Phase 1 SLSQP parent
    t1 = t0 + 0.22*safe
    if time.time() < t1:
       h,c,n = slsqp_polish(best_h,best_n,min(t1,end-10),seed,rep=2)
       if c < best_norm: update
    # Phase 2 pair parent
    t2 = t0 + 0.50*safe
    if time.time() < t2:
       h,c,n = pair_search(best_h,best_n,min(t2,end-10),seed+1,K=... Q=...)
       if c < best_norm: update
    # Phase 3 SLSQP high
    t3 = t0 + 0.62*safe
    if time.time() < t3:
       h,c,n = slsqp_polish(best_h,best_n,min(t3,end-10),seed+2, alphas=[100,400,1500], reps=2)
       update
    # Phase 4 pair
    t4 = t0 + 0.75*safe
    if time.time() < t4:
       h,c,n = pair_search(best_h,best_n,min(t4,end-10),seed+3, K=...,Q=...)
       update
    # Phase 5 exploration resolutions
    t_exp = end - 60
    if t_exp > t0 + 0.8*safe and time.time() < t_exp:
       candidates=[]
       base=best_n
       for factor in [0.4,0.6,0.8,1.0,1.25,1.5,2.0]:
          nn=int(base*factor)
          nn=int(round(nn)); if nn%2==1: nn+=1
          if 40 <= nn <= 1200: add
       for fixed in [60,100,200,300,500]: add
       # sort by abs(log nn/base) + np? 
       for nn in candidates unique:
          if time.time() > end - 80: break
          slice_len = 50.0 # or min
          if time.time() + slice_len > end - 40: slice_len = time.time()? 
          dl = min(end-50, time.time()+slice_len)
          if dl - time.time() < 25: continue
          h2,c2,n2 = resolution_trial(best_h,best_n,nn,dl,seed+10+nn%100)
          if c2 < best_norm: update
          # if best expanded maybe continue candidates? okay
    # Phase 6 final pair on best
    final_dl = end - 2
    if time.time() < final_dl:
       h,c,n = pair_search(best_h,best_n,final_dl,seed+99)
       update
    # final SLSQP tiny if time? maybe not
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    final_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, final_c, best_n
```

Let's design `project_feasible` robust and fast:
```
def project_feasible(z,n):
    x = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        x = np.resize(x,n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.full(n,0.5)
    target = 0.5*n
    # if x within bounds and close sum, simple? general
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    # If target <=0 return zeros etc not needed
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    lam = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    h = np.clip(x - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
    # residual loop
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        interior = (h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(interior))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[interior] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            # distribute to all
            if diff > 0:
                room = 1.0 - h
                rs = room.sum()
                if rs > 1e-14:
                    h += (diff/rs)*room
                else:
                    h[0] = 1.0
            else:
                ss = h.sum()
                if ss > 1e-14:
                    h += (diff/ss)*h
                else:
                    h[0] = 0.0
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final exact compensation on first interior/all
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 0:
        interior = (h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        if interior.sum() > 0:
            h[interior] += diff/interior.sum()
        else:
            h[n//2] = np.clip(h[n//2] + diff,0,1)
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Post final correction might break sum by clip if no room. But if no interior and target not integer, impossible exact. But n likely even. If n even target integer and values binary, no diff. If continuous, interior. Good.

```
def initial_fallback(seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n=300
    # start balanced random binary
    h=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx=rng.choice(n, n//2, replace=False)
    h[idx]=1.0
    # add noise? maybe project
    h = project_feasible(h + 0.1*rng.randn(n), n)

```

```
        return np.asarray(fftconvolve(a,b), dtype=float)
    return np.convolve(a,b,'full')
```

```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
       h=h[:n] if h.size>=n else np.resize(h,n)
    dx=2.0/float(n)
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
If h length not n? Should not. Use n=len(h) maybe.

`project_feasible` as above. Need if n=0? not.

```
def c_unnorm_direct(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return np.convolve(h, (1.0-h)[::-1], 'full')
```

`smooth_unnorm`:
```
def smooth_unnorm(h,alpha):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(h.shape)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    c=_conv(h, (1.0-h)[::-1])
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
       return m, np.zeros_like(h)
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    # if zm not finite? 
    expz=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=float(expz.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros_like(c)
       w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
       w=expz/s
    g=weighted_gradient(h,w)
    obj=m + float(np.log(max(s,1e-300))/alpha)
    if not np.isfinite(obj): obj=m
    return obj,g
```

`weighted_gradient`:
```
def weighted_gradient(h,w):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1
    w=np.asarray(w,dtype=float).ravel()
    if w.size != L:
       w = np.resize(w,L)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
       return np.zeros(n)
    total=float(w.sum())
    if total <= 0.0:
       return np.zeros(n)
    w = w / total
    B = w[::-1]
    pref=np.empty(L+1,dtype=float); pref[0]=0.0; np.cumsum(B, out=pref[1:])
    starts=np.arange(n-1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    ends=starts + (n-1)
    V=pref[ends+1]-pref[starts]
    D2=_conv(h,B)
    S2=D2[n-1:2*n-1]
    D1=_conv(h[::-1],B)
    S1=D1[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    g=V - S1 - S2
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       g=np.nan_to_num(g)
    return g
```
Need if n=1: L=1. starts arange(0,-1,-1) -> [0], ends [0], pref length2, V works. D2 length1, slice 0:1. h[::-1]. good.

`_obj_jac`, constraints:
```
def _obj_jac(x):
    if _OPT_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.time() > _OPT_DEADLINE:
        raise TimeLimit()
    return smooth_unnorm(x, _OPT_ALPHA)
def _constraint_fun(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _OPT_TARGET)
def _constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_OPT_N, dtype=float)
```
If `_OPT_N=0` returns empty. Fine.

`run_slsqp`:
```
def run_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    global _OPT_ALPHA,_OPT_N,_OPT_TARGET,_OPT_DEADLINE
    x0=project_feasible(h,n)
    _OPT_ALPHA=float(alpha); _OPT_N=int(n); _OPT_TARGET=0.5*n; _OPT_DEADLINE=float(deadline)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*int(n)
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_constraint_fun,'jac':_constraint_jac}]
    opts={'maxiter':int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-15, 'disp':False}
    x1=x0
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj_jac, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=True, options=opts)
       x1=res.x
    except Exception:
       x1=x0
    _OPT_DEADLINE=0.0
    return project_feasible(x1,n)
```
If `_obj_jac` returns tuple, minimize with jac=True expects. Good. But if smooth_unnorm raises? not.

Potential problem: `minimize` may call `_obj_jac` and `_constraint_fun` with x length n but if n=0? no.

`top_active`, `active_gradient`, `build_pools`, `optimize_t`.

`active_gradient`: Need if c max maybe use `cmax=float(np.max(c))`.
```
def active_gradient(h,c,active):
    n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n)
    m=len(active)
    if m == 0 or n ==0: return grad
    w=np.full(m,1.0/m)
    for pos in range(m):
       p=int(active[pos]); wt=float(w[pos])
       d=n-1-p
       if wt == 0: continue
       if d >=0:
          st=0; en=n-1-d
       else:
          st=-d; en=n-1
       grad[st:en+1] += wt * (1.0 - h[st+d:en+d+1])
       if d >=0:
          st2=d; en2=n-1
       else:
          st2=0; en2=n-1+d
       grad[st2:en2+1] -= wt * h[st2-d:en2-d+1]
    return grad
```
For d negative, `st2-d` where st2=0? If d=-1, st2=0, en=n-2, h[0: n-1]? st2-d =1, slice 1:n? yes. For d=-1, minus range x 0..n-2, h[x-d]=h[x+1] => h[1:n]. correct. For d=-1, plus start=1,end=n-1; h[start+d] = h[1-1=0: n] h[0:n-1]. correct.
If d large, slices lengths. Good.

`build_pools`:
```
def build_pools(v,h,rng,Q,n):
   Q = int(max(1, min(Q, n)))
   pos_parts=[]; neg_parts=[]
   if Q < n:
      pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, -Q)[-Q:])
      neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, Q)[:Q])
      pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, Q)[:Q])
      neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, n-Q)[-Q:])
      if 2*Q < n:
         pos_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False))
         neg_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False))
   else:
      allidx=np.arange(n)
      pos_parts.append(allidx)
      neg_parts.append(allidx)
   pos=np.unique(np.concatenate(pos_parts)).astype(int)
   neg=np.unique(np.concatenate(neg_parts)).astype(int)
   cap=32
   if len(pos) > cap: pos=rng.choice(pos, cap, replace=False)
   if len(neg) > cap: neg=rng.choice(neg, cap, replace=False)
   return pos,neg
```
If n small and rng.choice with replace=False size > len? We cap only if len>cap; but initial parts use Q<n. If n=1, Q=1 branch else allidx. pos len1 cap no. If n=2 Q maybe1. okay.
`np.argpartition(v, -Q)` when -Q negative and Q=n? branch Q<n; if Q=n? all. Good.
`np.argpartition(v, Q)` if Q=0 no; Q>=1.

`optimize_t`:
```
def optimize_t(low, high, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2, iterations=20):
    if high - low <= 1e-14:
       return 0.5*(low+high)
    def? no closure. Need top-level eval. 
```
We need top-level eval function. But it will be called many; pass arrays.
```
def _pair_phi(t, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2):
    if c_act.size:
       val=float(np.max(c_act + t*A_act))
    else:
       val=-np.inf
    t2=t*t
    v=c1 + t*A1 + t2
    if v > val: val=v
    v=c2 + t*A2 + t2
    if v > val: val=v
    return val
```
No closures.
`optimize_t` calls `_pair_phi`.
```
gr=0.6180339887498949
a=low; b=high
x1=a+(1-gr)*(b-a); x2=a+gr*(b-a)
f1=_pair_phi(...); f2=...
for _ in range(iterations):
   if f1 < f2:
      b=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=a+(1-gr)*(b-a); f1=_pair_phi(...)
   else:
      a=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=a+gr*(b-a); f2=_pair_phi(...)
t=(a+b)*0.5
# check endpoints and zero
best=t; bf=_pair_phi(best,...)
for cand in (low,high):
   cf=_pair_phi(cand,...); if cf<bf: best=cand; bf=cf
if low < 0.0 < high:
   cf=_pair_phi(0.0,...); if cf<bf: best=0.0
return best
```

Pseudo full:
```
def pair_search(h,n,deadline,seed,K=None,Q=None):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_norm = best_c*(2.0/n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    L = 2*n-1
    if n < 2: return h, compute_c5(h,n), n
    if K is None: K = min(64, max(24, int(n//5)))
    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    if Q is None: Q = min(16, max(6, int(n//15)))
    Q = int(max(1, min(Q, n)))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    active = top_active(c,K)
    r0 = 1.0 - h[::-1]
    work = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    A = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    tol_norm = 1e-12
    empty_sweeps = 0
    long_no = 0
    accept_count = 0
    max_sweeps = 100000
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       if empty_sweeps >= 3: break
       if long_no > 30000: break
       active = top_active(c,K)
       grad = active_gradient(h,c,active)
       v = -grad
       v = v - float(np.mean(v))
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v = np.zeros(n)
       pos, neg = build_pools(v,h,rng,Q,n)
       pairs=[]
       max_pairs=1200
       for i in pos:
          for j in neg:
             if i == j: continue
             pairs.append((int(i),int(j)))
             if len(pairs) >= max_pairs: break
          if len(pairs) >= max_pairs: break
       nr = int(max(20, 3*Q))
       for _ in range(nr):
          i = int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
          if i == j: continue
          pairs.append((i,j))
          if len(pairs) >= max_pairs+50: break
       sweep_improved = False
       for i,j in pairs:
          if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
          hi = float(h[i]); hj=float(h[j])
          low=max(-hi, hj-1.0); high=min(hj, 1.0-hi)
          if high - low <= 1e-13: continue
          A.fill(0.0)
          A[i:i+n] += r0
          A[j:j+n] -= r0
          A[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
          A[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
          dy=j-i
          p1=n-1-dy; p2=n-1+dy
          c_act = c[active].copy()
          A_act = A[active].copy()
          c1=float(c[p1]); A1=float(A[p1]); c2=float(c[p2]); A2=float(A[p2])
          t=optimize_t(low,high,c_act,A_act,c1,A1,c2,A2, iterations=18)
          if t < low: t=low
          if t > high: t=high
          if abs(t) <= 1e-15: continue
          t2=t*t
          np.multiply(A, t, out=work)
          np.add(c, work, out=work)
          work[p1] += t2
          if p2 != p1: work[p2] += t2
          newmax=float(np.max(work))
          if newmax * (2.0/n) < best_norm - tol_norm:
              hi_new=hi+t; hj_new=hj-t
              if hi_new < -1e-10 or hi_new > 1.0+1e-10 or hj_new < -1e-10 or hj_new > 1.0+1e-10:
                  h[i]=min(1.0,max(0.0,hi_new)); h[j]=min(1.0,max(0.0,hj_new))
                  c=c_unnorm_direct(h)
                  mc=float(np.max(c))
                  best_norm=mc*(2.0/n)
                  best_c=mc
                  best_h=h.copy()
                  r0=1.0-h[::-1]
                  active=top_active(c,K)
              else:
                  h[i]=hi_new; h[j]=hj_new
                  np.copyto(c, work)
                  best_c=newmax; best_norm=newmax*(2.0/n)
                  best_h=h.copy()
                  r0[n-1-i] -= t
                  r0[n-1-j] += t
              accept_count += 1
              sweep_improved=True
              long_no=0
              if accept_count % 10 == 0:
                  active=top_active(c,K)
              if accept_count % 50 == 0:
                  c2c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
                  mc=float(np.max(c2c))
                  if mc*(2.0/n) < best_norm: # due to recompute? it should be close. If recompute better, update; if worse due to drift, maybe revert? Hard.
                  # We cannot easily revert. If recompute is worse, we should set best to previous best_h? But c currently after move might be slightly worse due to numerical but actual move improved. Keep? To be safe, if recompute norm > best_norm + 1e-10, set h/c to best_h? But best_h copied before? We can revert.
```

Now, `slsqp_polish` with `reps` parameter:
```
def slsqp_polish(h,n,deadline,seed=0,alphas=None,maxiter=None,reps=2):
   best_h = project_feasible(h,n)
   best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
   rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
   if alphas is None: alphas=[10,50,200,800]
   if maxiter is None:
      if n <= 300: maxiter=150
      elif n<=600: maxiter=80
      else: maxiter=40
   for rep in range(int(reps)):
      for alpha in alphas:
         if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
         h_try = best_h
         if rep > 0:
            scale = 0.02/(1.0+rep)
            h_try = project_feasible(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), n)
            ct = compute_c5(h_try,n)
            if ct < best_c:
               best_c=ct; best_h=h_try.copy()
         # if no time enough, still run with deadline maybe 10s; could raise
         if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
         h1 = run_slsqp(h_try,n,alpha,maxiter, deadline - 2)
         if h1 is not None:
            c1 = compute_c5(h1,n)
            if c1 < best_c - 1e-15:
               best_c=c1; best_h=h1.copy()
      if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
   return best_h,best_c,n
```
If `run_slsqp` raises TimeLimit and returns h0, no update. Good.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new):
   h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
   if n_new == n_old:
      return project_feasible(h,n_new)
   if h.size != n_old:
      h = np.resize(h,n_old)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old,endpoint=False)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new,endpoint=False)
   hh=np.interp(x_new,x_old,h,left=0.0,right=0.0)
   # If nearly constant, set 0.5? no
   return project_feasible(hh,n_new)
```
If n_old=1, linspace one. okay.

`random_candidate(n,seed)`:
```
def random_binary(n, seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   h=np.zeros(n)
   target=n//2
   if target>0 and target<n:
      idx=rng.choice(n,target,replace=False)
      h[idx]=1.0
   return h
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None

_OPT_ALPHA = 1.0
_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_TARGET = 0.0
_OPT_DEADLINE = 0.0


class TimeLimit(Exception):
    pass


def _conv(a, b):
    if fftconvolve is not None and len(a) * len(b) > 1200000:
        r = fftconvolve(a, b)
        if np.iscomplexobj(r):
            r = r.real
        return np.asarray(r, dtype=float)
    return np.convolve(a, b, "full")


def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size > n:
            h = h[:n]
        else:
            h = np.resize(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def project_feasible(z, n):
    x = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        if x.size > n:
            x = x[:n]
        else:
            x = np.resize(x, n)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    if float(np.min(x)) >= -1e-8 and float(np.max(x)) <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
        h = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-8:
            for _ in range(4):
                diff = target - float(h.sum())
                if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
                    break
                interior = (h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
                cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(interior))
                if cnt > 0:
                    h[interior] += diff / float(cnt)
                else:
                    if diff > 0.0:
                        room = 1.0 - h
                        rs = float(room.sum())
                        if rs > 1e-14:
                            h += (diff / rs) * room
                    else:
                        ss = float(h.sum())
                        if ss > 1e-14:
                            h += (diff / ss) * h
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
            return h

    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(50):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(x - lam, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        interior = (h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(interior))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[interior] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            if diff > 0.0:
                room = 1.0 - h
                rs = float(room.sum())
                if rs > 1e-14:
                    h += (diff / rs) * room
                else:
                    h[0] = 1.0
            else:
                ss = float(h.sum())
                if ss > 1e-14:
                    h += (diff / ss) * h
                else:
                    h[0] = 0.0
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 0.0:
        interior = (h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(interior))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[interior] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            idx = min(n - 1, max(0, n // 2))
            h[idx] = float(np.clip(h[idx] + diff, 0.0, 1.0))

    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def c_unnorm_direct(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return np.convolve(h, (1.0 - h)[::-1], "full")


def weighted_gradient(h, w):
    n = len(h)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).ravel()
    if w.size != L:
        w = np.resize(w, L)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    total = float(np.sum(w))
    if total <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    w = w / total
    B = w[::-1]

    pref = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(B, out=pref[1:])

    starts = np.arange(n - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    ends = starts + (n - 1)
    V = pref[ends + 1] - pref[starts]

    D2 = _conv(h, B)
    S2 = D2[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    D1 = _conv(h[::-1], B)
    S1 = D1[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    g = V - S1 - S2
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.nan_to_num(g)
    return g


def smooth_unnorm(h, alpha):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(h, dtype=float)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    c = _conv(h, (1.0 - h)[::-1])
    m = float(np.max(c))

    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        return m, np.zeros_like(h, dtype=float)

    z = alpha * (c - m)
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    expz = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(np.sum(expz))

    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wp = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        wp[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        wp = expz / s

    g = weighted_gradient(h, wp)
    obj = m + float(np.log(max(s, 1e-300)) / alpha)
    if not np.isfinite(obj):
        obj = m
    return obj, g


def _obj_jac(x):
    if _OPT_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.time() > _OPT_DEADLINE:
        raise TimeLimit()
    return smooth_unnorm(x, _OPT_ALPHA)


def _constraint_fun(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _OPT_TARGET)


def _constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_OPT_N, dtype=float)


def run_slsqp(h, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline):
    global _OPT_ALPHA, _OPT_N, _OPT_TARGET, _OPT_DEADLINE

    x0 = project_feasible(h, n)

    _OPT_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    _OPT_N = int(n)
    _OPT_TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)
    _OPT_DEADLINE = float(deadline)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _constraint_fun, "jac": _constraint_jac}]
    opts = {"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-15, "disp": False}

    x1 = x0
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj_jac,
            x0,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=True,
            options=opts,
        )
        x1 = res.x
    except Exception:
        x1 = x0

    _OPT_DEADLINE = 0.0
    return project_feasible(x1, n)


def top_active(c, K):
    L = len(c)
    K = int(max(0, min(K, L)))
    if K <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if K >= L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=int)
    return np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:].astype(int)


def active_gradient(h, c, active):
    n = len(h)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = int(len(active))
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return grad

    w = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)

    for pos in range(m):
        p = int(active[pos])
        wt = float(w[pos])
        if wt == 0.0:
            continue
        d = n - 1 - p

        if d >= 0:
            st = 0
            en = n - 1 - d
        else:
            st = -d
            en = n - 1
        if en >= st:
            grad[st:en + 1] += wt * (1.0 - h[st + d:en + d + 1])

        if d >= 0:
            st2 = d
            en2 = n - 1
        else:
            st2 = 0
            en2 = n - 1 + d
        if en2 >= st2:
            grad[st2:en2 + 1] -= wt * h[st2 - d:en2 - d + 1]

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad)
    return grad


def build_pools(v, h, rng, Q, n):
    Q = int(max(1, min(Q, n)))
    pos_parts = []
    neg_parts = []

    if Q < n:
        pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, -Q)[-Q:].astype(int))
        neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(v, Q)[:Q].astype(int))

        pos_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, Q)[:Q].astype(int))
        neg_parts.append(np.argpartition(h, n - Q)[-Q:].astype(int))

        if 2 * Q < n:
            pos_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False).astype(int))
            neg_parts.append(rng.choice(n, Q, replace=False).astype(int))
    else:
        allidx = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
        pos_parts.append(allidx)
        neg_parts.append(allidx)

    pos = np.unique(np.concatenate(pos_parts)).astype(int)
    neg = np.unique(np.concatenate(neg_parts)).astype(int)

    cap = 32
    if len(pos) > cap:
        pos = rng.choice(pos, cap, replace=False).astype(int)
    if len(neg) > cap:
        neg = rng.choice(neg, cap, replace=False).astype(int)

    return pos, neg


def _pair_phi(t, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2):
    if c_act.size:
        val = float(np.max(c_act + t * A_act))
    else:
        val = -np.inf
    t2 = t * t
    v1 = c1 + t * A1 + t2
    if v1 > val:
        val = v1
    v2 = c2 + t * A2 + t2
    if v2 > val:
        val = v2
    return val


def optimize_t(low, high, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2, iterations=18):
    if high - low <= 1e-14:
        return 0.5 * (low + high)

    gr = 0.6180339887498949
    a = float(low)
    b = float(high)

    x1 = a + (1.0 - gr) * (b - a)
    x2 = a + gr * (b - a)
    f1 = _pair_phi(x1, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)
    f2 = _pair_phi(x2, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)

    for _ in range(int(iterations)):
        if f1 < f2:
            b = x2
            x2 = x1
            f2 = f1
            x1 = a + (1.0 - gr) * (b - a)
            f1 = _pair_phi(x1, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)
        else:
            a = x1
            x1 = x2
            f1 = f2
            x2 = a + gr * (b - a)
            f2 = _pair_phi(x2, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)

    t = 0.5 * (a + b)
    best = t
    bf = _pair_phi(best, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)

    for cand in (low, high):
        cf = _pair_phi(cand, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)
        if cf < bf:
            best = cand
            bf = cf

    if low < 0.0 < high:
        cf = _pair_phi(0.0, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2)
        if cf < bf:
            best = 0.0

    return float(best)


def pair_search(h, n, deadline, seed, K=None, Q=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).astype(float).copy()
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return h, compute_c5(h, n), n

    c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
    best_c = float(np.max(c))
    best_norm = best_c * (2.0 / float(n))
    best_h = h.copy()

    L = 2 * n - 1

    if K is None:
        K = min(64, max(24, int(n // 5)))
    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))

    if Q is None:
        Q = min(16, max(6, int(n // 15)))
    Q = int(max(1, min(Q, n)))

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    active = top_active(c, K)
    r0 = 1.0 - h[::-1]

    work = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    A = np.empty(L, dtype=float)

    tol_norm = 1e-12
    empty_sweeps = 0
    long_no = 0
    accept_count = 0
    max_sweeps = 100000

    for _sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time() > float(deadline):
            break
        if empty_sweeps >= 3:
            break
        if long_no > 30000:
            break

        active = top_active(c, K)
        grad = active_gradient(h, c, active)
        v = -grad
        v = v - float(np.mean(v))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

        pos, neg = build_pools(v, h, rng, Q, n)

        pairs = []
        max_pairs = 1200
        for i in pos:
            ii = int(i)
            for j in neg:
                jj = int(j)
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                pairs.append((ii, jj))
                if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                    break
            if len(pairs) >= max_pairs:
                break

        nr = int(max(20, 3 * Q))
        for _rr in range(nr):
            ii = int(rng.randint(n))
            jj = int(rng.randint(n))
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            pairs.append((ii, jj))
            if len(pairs) >= max_pairs + 50:
                break

        sweep_improved = False

        for i, j in pairs:
            if time.time() > float(deadline) - 0.5:
                break

            hi = float(h[i])
            hj = float(h[j])

            low = max(-hi, hj - 1.0)
            high = min(hj, 1.0 - hi)
            if high - low <= 1e-13:
                continue

            A.fill(0.0)
            A[i:i + n] += r0
            A[j:j + n] -= r0
            A[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i] -= h
            A[n - 1 - j:2 * n - 1 - j] += h

            dy = j - i
            p1 = n - 1 - dy
            p2 = n - 1 + dy

            c_act = c[active].copy()
            A_act = A[active].copy()
            c1 = float(c[p1])
            A1 = float(A[p1])
            c2 = float(c[p2])
            A2 = float(A[p2])

            t = optimize_t(low, high, c_act, A_act, c1, A1, c2, A2, iterations=18)
            if t < low:
                t = low
            if t > high:
                t = high
            if abs(t) <= 1e-15:
                continue

            t2 = t * t
            np.multiply(A, t, out=work)
            np.add(c, work, out=work)
            work[p1] += t2
            if p2 != p1:
                work[p2] += t2

            newmax = float(np.max(work))
            if newmax * (2.0 / float(n)) < best_norm - tol_norm:
                hi_new = hi + t
                hj_new = hj - t

                if hi_new < -1e-12 or hi_new > 1.0 + 1e-12 or hj_new < -1e-12 or hj_new > 1.0 + 1e-12:
                    continue

                if hi_new < 0.0:
                    hi_new = 0.0
                elif hi_new > 1.0:
                    hi_new = 1.0
                if hj_new < 0.0:
                    hj_new = 0.0
                elif hj_new > 1.0:
                    hj_new = 1.0

                di = hi_new - hi
                dj = hj_new - hj

                if abs(di + dj) > 1e-10:
                    continue

                h[i] = hi_new
                h[j] = hj_new
                np.copyto(c, work)

                best_c = newmax
                best_norm = newmax * (2.0 / float(n))
                best_h = h.copy()

                r0[n - 1 - i] -= di
                r0[n - 1 - j] -= dj

                accept_count += 1
                sweep_improved = True
                long_no = 0

                if accept_count % 10 == 0:
                    active = top_active(c, K)

                if accept_count % 50 == 0:
                    c = c_unnorm_direct(h)
                    mc = float(np.max(c))
                    best_c = mc
                    best_norm = mc * (2.0 / float(n))
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    active = top_active(c, K)
                    r0 = 1.0 - h[::-1]
            else:
                long_no += 1

        if sweep_improved:
            empty_sweeps = 0
        else:
            empty_sweeps += 1

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    cur_h = project_feasible(h, n)

    best_norm2 = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    cur_norm = compute_c5(cur_h, n)

    if cur_norm < best_norm2:
        best_h = cur_h
        best_norm2 = cur_norm

    return best_h, best_norm2, n


def slsqp_polish(h, n, deadline, seed=0, alphas=None, maxiter=None, reps=2):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    if alphas is None:
        alphas = [10.0, 60.0, 300.0]

    if maxiter is None:
        if n <= 300:
            maxiter = 160
        elif n <= 600:
            maxiter = 80
        else:
            maxiter = 40

    reps = int(max(0, reps))

    for rep in range(reps):
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > float(deadline) - 8.0:
                break

            h_try = best_h
            if rep > 0:
                scale = 0.02 / float(1 + rep)
                h_try = project_feasible(best_h + scale * rng.randn(n), n)
                ct = compute_c5(h_try, n)
                if ct < best_c:
                    best_c = ct
                    best_h = h_try.copy()

            if time.time() > float(deadline) - 6.0:
                break

            h1 = run_slsqp(h_try, n, alpha, maxiter, deadline - 2.0)
            if h1 is not None:
                c1 = compute_c5(h1, n)
                if c1 < best_c - 1e-15:
                    best_c = c1
                    best_h = h1.copy()

        if time.time() > float(deadline) - 12.0:
            break

    return best_h, best_c, n


def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n_old = int(n_old)
    n_new = int(n_new)

    if h.size != n_old:
        if h.size > n_old:
            h = h[:n_old]
        else:
            h = np.resize(h, n_old)

    if n_new == n_old:
        return project_feasible(h, n_new)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    hh = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return project_feasible(hh, n_new)


def random_binary(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    if target > 0 and target < n:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
        idx = rng.choice(n, target, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def resolution_trial(base_h, base_n, new_n, deadline, seed):
    new_n = int(max(4, new_n))
    base_n = int(base_n)

    h1 = resample_h(base_h, base_n, new_n)
    c1 = compute_c5(h1, new_n)

    h2 = random_binary(new_n, seed)
    c2 = compute_c5(h2, new_n)
    if c2 < c1:
        h1 = h2
        c1 = c2

    now = time.time()
    total = max(0.0, float(deadline) - now)
    if total > 30.0:
        sl_dead = now + total * 0.40
        if total > 120.0:
            sl_dead = min(sl_dead, now + 70.0)
        h1, c1, _ = slsqp_polish(
            h1,
            new_n,
            sl_dead,
            seed,
            alphas=[20.0, 100.0, 400.0],
            maxiter=55,
            reps=1,
        )
        h3, c3, _ = pair_search(h1, new_n, deadline, seed + 1)
        if c3 < c1:
            return h3, c3, new_n

    return h1, c1, new_n


def initial_fallback(seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    n = 300
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = rng.choice(n, n // 2, replace=False)
    h[idx] = 1.0
    h = project_feasible(h + 0.1 * rng.randn(n), n)
    return h


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    safe = max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    end = t0 + safe

    try:
        h_init = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h_init.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h_init)):
            raise ValueError("bad parent")
    except Exception:
        h_init = initial_fallback(seed)

    n = int(len(h_init))

    if n > 1400:
        h_init = resample_h(h_init, n, 1400)
        n = 1400

    h_init = project_feasible(h_init, n)

    best_h = h_init.copy()
    best_n = n
    best_norm = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    try:
        # 1) SLSQP from the parent scale.
        t1 = t0 + 0.22 * safe
        if time.time() < t1:
            hh, cc, nn = slsqp_polish(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                min(t1, end - 10.0),
                seed,
                alphas=[8.0, 40.0, 150.0],
                maxiter=170,
                reps=1,
            )
            cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
            if cc < best_norm:
                best_h = hh
                best_norm = cc
                best_n = nn

        # 2) Pairwise coordinate polishing.
        t2 = t0 + 0.50 * safe
        if time.time() < t2:
            hh, cc, nn = pair_search(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                min(t2, end - 10.0),
                seed + 1,
            )
            cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
            if cc < best_norm:
                best_h = hh
                best_norm = cc
                best_n = nn

        # 3) Higher-temperature SLSQP polish with sharpened max.
        t3 = t0 + 0.62 * safe
        if time.time() < t3:
            hh, cc, nn = slsqp_polish(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                min(t3, end - 12.0),
                seed + 2,
                alphas=[250.0, 1200.0, 5000.0],
                maxiter=110,
                reps=2,
            )
            cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
            if cc < best_norm:
                best_h = hh
                best_norm = cc
                best_n = nn

        # 4) More dual-like local moves.
        t4 = t0 + 0.75 * safe
        if time.time() < t4:
            hh, cc, nn = pair_search(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                min(t4, end - 15.0),
                seed + 3,
            )
            cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
            if cc < best_norm:
                best_h = hh
                best_norm = cc
                best_n = nn

        # 5) Try a few neighboring resolutions.
        exp_end = min(end - 45.0, t0 + 0.92 * safe)
        if time.time() < exp_end - 35.0:
            cand = set()

            base = float(best_n)
            for factor in (0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0):
                nn = int(round(base * factor))
                if nn % 2 == 1:
                    nn += 1
                if 40 <= nn <= 1200:
                    cand.add(nn)

            for fixed in (60, 100, 150, 200, 300, 400, 500, 800):
                if 40 <= fixed <= 1200:
                    cand.add(fixed)

            items = []
            for nn in cand:
                items.append((abs(float(nn) - float(best_n)), int(nn)))
            items.sort()

            trials = 0
            for _, nn in items:
                if time.time() > exp_end - 25.0 or trials >= 4:
                    break
                remaining = exp_end - time.time()
                if remaining < 55.0:
                    break
                slice_len = min(70.0, remaining - 25.0)
                if slice_len < 25.0:
                    break
                dl = time.time() + slice_len
                if dl > exp_end - 20.0:
                    dl = exp_end - 20.0

                hh, cc, nn = resolution_trial(best_h, best_n, nn, dl, seed + 20 + trials)
                cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
                if cc < best_norm:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_norm = cc
                    best_n = nn
                trials += 1

        # 6) Final pair polish.
        final_dl = end - 4.0
        if time.time() < final_dl:
            hh, cc, nn = pair_search(
                best_h,
                best_n,
                final_dl,
                seed + 99,
            )
            cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
            if cc < best_norm:
                best_h = hh
                best_norm = cc
                best_n = nn

    except Exception:
        pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_c = float(best_norm)

    return best_h, final_c, best_n
```
